# DeliDev implementation and evidence ledger

Issue #964 is preserved in full in [requirements](cmds-delidev-requirements.md). This ledger distinguishes code, deterministic tests, and actual external/native evidence. It is not a reduction of the requirements.

## Current implementation work
The CLI/server/Worker implementation is in progress. No release or complete real-account harness integration claim is made; bounded native evidence is recorded separately below.

## Presentation scope
The owner explicitly confirmed on 2026-09-25 that the active request includes the desktop app and the complete issue #964. The earlier CLI-only scope describes prior implementation increments, not the current completion criterion. Desktop windows/tray/widgets/native browser presentation, artifact installation and all remaining server/Worker requirements still need implementation and real evidence; no desktop completion is claimed.

The TypeScript direct-Connect integration boundary is now implemented in `packages/delidev-api-client`, with generated service-specific React Query descriptors and bounded snapshot/event synchronization. Its real Go-server fixture validates the transport and replay boundary; it does not establish a packaged desktop or large-history presentation.

## Local verification log
- 2026-09-24, macOS arm64, Go 1.26.1: `go test -race ./cmds/delidev-cli/internal/...` and `go vet ./cmds/delidev-cli/internal/...` for the domain, private-files, and SQLite foundation. No harness or provider account was invoked.
- 2026-09-24: `go test -race ./cmds/delidev-cli/...`, package vet, Buf formatting/lint, and generated Go bindings validated. The native macOS executable was exercised against an isolated temporary data directory. Linux arm64 cross-compilation succeeded; this does not establish native Linux behavior.
- 2026-09-24: Windows amd64 cross-compilation succeeded. Native Windows runtime remains unverified. Real temporary Git preparation tests and package race tests/vet pass on macOS arm64.
- A subsequent Git-registration regression exposed the macOS `/var` parent alias during rollback. Worker roots now canonicalize before ownership journals are created; the real Git regression and package race/vet checks were rerun after the fix.

## Native interface discovery (not execution validation)
- Installed read-only version/help checks: Codex CLI `0.151.0`, Claude Code `2.1.236`, OpenCode `1.18.20`. No `grok` executable was found on this machine. No user credential files were read and no inference was invoked by these checks.
- Official adapter references retrieved: [Codex app-server](https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/app-server) for native expected-turn steering; [Claude Code programmatic execution](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/headless) for structured streams; [OpenCode server](https://opencode.ai/docs/server/) for authenticated native HTTP; [Grok Build headless/ACP](https://docs.x.ai/build/cli/headless-scripting) for native JSON-RPC integration. Installed-version capability validation and real-account evidence remain required before claiming an adapter supported end to end.

- Worker schema v2 migration preserves a consistent private v1 backup before changes; real SQLite tests cover successful upgrade and rollback after a conflicting migration. Revocation tests reject both new mutations and receipt replays from a previously authenticated principal.

- CLI integration tests pair both a Worker and a client, reuse pairing receipts, inspect a real Git subdirectory over outbound Connect, and reject Worker credentials for owner resource queries. Package race tests and vet pass after this integration.

- Native macOS CLI subprocess smoke passed for explicit server startup, private Worker pairing, outbound readiness, real Git inspection, asynchronous repository save, and revocation shutting down the Worker with a typed authentication error. No network fetch, inference or user account access was involved.

- Revocation now atomically cancels undispatched Worker jobs and preserves claimed-job uncertainty; real Connect regressions cover both states. Migration backup names are synchronized before schema commit. Final package race tests/vet and generated-binding freshness checks pass; updated Worker CLI cross-compiles for Windows amd64 and Linux arm64 without implying native runtime validation.

- Native macOS process tests cover start-before-side-effect barriers, separate stdout/stderr, byte-split UTF-8 stdin, blocked-input cancellation, daemonized descendants after natural exit/cancel/disconnect, supervisor-death recovery, cross-owner refusal, unrelated live scopes, missing journals, output-consumer failure and secret-free ownership journals. Worker Git now uses these scopes and retains cleanup uncertainty. Linux/Windows process test binaries cross-compile; native Linux/Windows lifecycle and PTY behavior remain unverified.

- Worker discovery now runs end to end through `machine discover`, outbound jobs and atomic machine publication. Real Connect tests cover authorization, exact request/result retries, stale discovery generations, independent attachment revisions, unverified-capability rejection, safe diagnostic reconstruction and rollback after a wrong job revision. Temporary native process fixtures verify environment isolation, explicit-path precedence, missing/permission/incompatible outcomes, bounded raw output, cancellation and confirmed runtime cleanup. Native launch errors retain safe OS classifications.
- Native macOS arm64 CLI/server/Worker smoke, isolated temporary server and Worker directories: version discovery observed Codex `0.151.0`, Claude Code `2.1.236`, OpenCode `1.18.20`, and missing Grok Build. All observations correctly retained `protocol_verified=false`. No account login, inference, native protocol handshake, or model availability claim follows from this smoke. Probe processes and private runtime directories were cleaned after native ownership reconciliation.

- After discovery integration: complete delidev Go race tests and vet pass, Buf lint passes, and regeneration reproduces all bindings exactly. Windows amd64 and Linux arm64 CLI builds and platform test-binary cross-compilation pass; native Windows/Linux discovery and lifecycle remain unverified. No repository-owned generated `dist` directory remains.

- Native JSON-RPC fixtures cover split Unicode frames, typed/redacted native errors, concurrent interaction replies, pre-send cancellation, uncertain request delivery and late acknowledgments, blocked input, malformed/bounded frames, slow-consumer overflow, and confirmed owned-process cleanup. Protocol-profile tests reject changed native versions, foreign homes/platforms, extra response fields and preexisting loaded threads without invoking inference.
- Native macOS arm64 CLI/server/Worker `machine discover --protocol` smoke successfully verified Codex app-server `0.151.0` initialization and empty bounded loaded-thread state. Actual native testing exposed the optional `emittedAtMs` notification metadata and `nextCursor` response field; both are now represented and covered by fixtures. The final probe left zero private probe directories after native reconciliation. Execution capabilities remain empty, and no account login, thread creation, model refresh or inference was performed. Claude Code/OpenCode protocol profiles and missing Grok remain explicitly unverified.

- After native protocol integration, complete delidev race tests and vet pass; the legacy discovery-receipt regression also passes after closing/reopening real SQLite. Buf lint and exact regeneration checks pass. Updated Windows amd64/Linux arm64 CLI binaries and native protocol test binaries cross-compile, without claiming native Windows/Linux or account execution verification. No repository-owned generated `dist` directory remains.

- Protected credential storage now implements native macOS Security.framework, Windows Credential Manager and Linux Secret Service wrapping-key adapters, with AES-256-GCM private envelopes, domain-separated commitment keys, durable staging/deletion markers and exact immutable retries. It is a storage primitive; account lifecycle RPC/CLI integration, upstream validation, login, proxy authorization and execution revocation remain pending.
- Native macOS arm64 tests on macOS 26.6.2 passed against a newly created temporary keychain, including duplicate refusal, locked-store failure without UI, temporary-keychain unlock, native deletion and larger encrypted payloads. Testing identified that file-keychain operations need the process-wide no-UI setting as well as the query option. No default/login keychain credentials were read.
- Native Linux arm64 credential tests passed in a network-disabled disposable Debian/GNOME Keyring container with a non-root user, private home and private D-Bus session. Real native testing exposed GNOME Keyring's `text/plain` metadata for binary values; the adapter now preserves and validates the binary payload independently of MIME. Actual create/read/duplicate/delete, larger envelopes and locked collection/deletion-marker behavior passed. This proves the Secret Service adapter in that container, not complete Linux desktop/Worker lifecycle acceptance.
- After credential storage changes, the complete delidev Go race suite and vet pass. Windows amd64/arm64, Linux amd64 and macOS amd64 credential test binaries cross-compile; native Windows execution remains unverified. Tests cover cancellation after a native write, uncertain writes before/after acceptance, restart reuse, conflicting concurrent writes, missing/corrupt keys and records, private-file rejection and deletion resurrection prevention. No repository-owned generated `dist` output was produced.

- API/keyless account lifecycle is integrated through authenticated Connect and the CLI. Failure injection uses a test-only secret backend; stored credentials stay unverified and cannot route.

### Schedule server coordinator
- The server now owns a bounded scheduling loop joined before storage/vault closure. - Overlap creates independent source-linked sessions; Skip and Wait inspect current sessions, including explicitly resumed/re-prepared historical runs. Wait preserves immutable accepted selections through edits/pause/deletion and restart, releases only its FIFO head, and remains queued across Worker disconnection. Invalid current references/Local origin become typed failed history before side effects. Offline due instants and wait-capacity overflow produce explicit skipped history without evicting prior acceptance or creating execution catch-up work.
- Completion reconciliation preserves native question/approval waiting, recovery, archive/preparation cleanup and pending automatic follow-up. Terminal native events alone cannot release successors. Exact completed job/input identity and owned cleanup are required, and original input/Steer/interaction records are independently checked so an uncertain response cannot hide behind a zero summary counter. Completed occurrence history stays immutable after later explicit session activity.

- Addressed 16 connector findings from the initial and final inventories with isolated commits and focused regressions: backup directory durability and atomic migration publication; failed fresh SQLite and initial vault-pin recovery; completed process-scope retirement; retained account-reference deletion guards; unsupported authentication observations; Git remote-HEAD failure propagation; protected SSE metadata/error codes; nonzero bounded-wait failures; current-process Worker schedule availability; encoded-size resource pagination; keyless cleanup without credential storage; stopped/archived schedule outcomes after late native success; and failed pre-launch execution-claim publication.

The Worker owns a fresh loopback server, random process-only Basic credential and exact reserved port, verifies the official SDK startup record and rejects absent/wrong credentials before accepting the original credential's health/version. Empty global configuration and bounded static native operation advertisements complete the probe. No TUI attachment, provider query, project/session initialization, login, prompt or hosted inference occurs. HTTP has closed routes/authority, no ambient proxy, redirect, compression or application retry; joined output/process cleanup precedes runtime removal.
- Verified official npm `opencode-darwin-arm64@1.18.32` against published SHA-512 `sDsHk/A6FNqeN5NuAiII6AAhi4pJIFcIY0S2yjG9gToIIVaqrQRaHQ6g0kpoVqTiMci8tvaND6tWD4eDT3ahFw==` in an external temporary fixture directory. Source references use official tag commit `545f51d26cc39a907d2867492d498d9607ea5fa4`. No global installation/update or user credential access was performed. Actual macOS arm64 global health returns 401 for missing/wrong authentication, 200 for the generated credential, version `1.18.32`, empty global config and a 478,968-byte OpenAPI document; the document's empty security metadata is not substituted for authentication checks.
- Actual production discovery initially refused the reused version-probe runtime because native `--version` creates XDG subdirectories. The final implementation allocates a separate fresh protocol runtime; executable fixtures reproduce that side effect. Both actual direct adapter and production discovery now pass, retaining no completed probe directory. Static operation advertisements remain distinct from native session semantics, project/managed configuration and plugin dependency authority, event reconnects, execution/accounts and the complete platform matrix.
- Validation: complete DeliDev Go tests and vet, OpenCode/harness/domain race tests, Windows amd64/Linux arm64 CLI cross-builds and twenty actual native repetitions each for the direct adapter and Worker discovery pass. HTTP fixtures additionally prove no proxy/redirect/foreign-authority request, no session-route access, exact credential scope, body/header/timeout refusal and schema-operation identity checks. No frontend, Rust or generated protocol schema changed.

### OpenCode original sessions and asynchronous input storage (2026-09-26)
- Added a separate package-private native session transport, without a discovery/public endpoint constructor. Creation pins its original request metadata, native session/project/slug/creation identity, workspace, agent/provider/model and ordered permission rules. Every mutation requires an exact metadata-only synchronized claim before its one HTTP attempt; callback/response loss never releases creation or input ownership. Closed routes, bounded no-retry HTTP and typed private diagnostics preserve native authority. This remains separate from the required production effective-runtime/account/relay initializer and public Worker integration.
- Original native message/part IDs accompany one asynchronous text input. HTTP scheduling acknowledgment and complete original user-message storage are separate retained facts; a lost response can be followed by exact original storage inspection without replay or invented HTTP acceptance. Missing records do not prove rejection, contradictory session reads retain a send block, and later disappearance cannot erase confirmed storage. No second-send, assistant completion, Steer, process replacement or general native interaction authority is inferred.
- Actual pinned macOS arm64 OpenCode `1.18.32` creates and reads sessions, calls a private scripted OpenAI-compatible provider and preserves original message/part IDs. Generated isolated runtime/credentials, absent managed policy, disabled extension scanners and offline native npm initialization avoid user accounts, global dependency installation and hosted inference. The provider response is held while storage is verified, directly demonstrating that scheduling/storage precede assistant completion. Claims are synchronized in temporary private files. Both normal and deliberately lost HTTP-response scenarios pass ten repeated native runs each.
- Repetition exposed a real non-atomic native storage observation: the exact user-message row can be visible with an empty part array before the original part is stored. The pinned official `SessionPrompt.createUserMessage` source independently confirms sequential message then part writes. The final adapter retains this valid intermediate state as unconfirmed, accepts only the later exact complete part and rejects regression after confirmation. Deterministic coverage reproduces both transitions; malformed/null/foreign/extra parts remain refused. An initial generic schema failure is therefore diagnosed and fixed, not hidden by delaying the fixture.
- Race validation also exposed an earlier discovery diagnostic-ordering bug: bounded output failure cancels HTTP, whose generic cancellation could be returned first. Joined cleanup now preserves the original typed stream violation after both drains finish. Final complete DeliDev Go tests and vet, the complete OpenCode race suite, Windows amd64/Linux arm64 CLI cross-builds and the twenty native session scenarios pass. No frontend, Rust or generated protocol schema changed; the complete issue and other harness/desktop integrations remain unfinished.

Focused domain/workspace tests pass (`/tmp/delidev-session-diff-third.log`).
- The ordinary server/Worker/CLI integration compares both real prepared repositories and validates their original creation bases and returned changes. Domain/workspace/server/Worker/CLI race tests pass (`/tmp/delidev-session-diff-race.log`), DeliDev-wide Go vet passes (`/tmp/delidev-session-diff-vet.log`), and the complete DeliDev Go suite passes (`/tmp/delidev-session-diff-full-go.log`). Existing stream ownership, revocation, malformed observation and native execution isolation regressions remain in these suites; these results do not establish other-platform or real hosted-account acceptance.
- A current macOS native verification bundle connects to its private retained server and paired Worker. Public CLI configuration creates a private repository/project and real detached Worktree; the native Diff panel displays its unstaged patch, distinct staged patch, original base/HEAD and separate untracked filename. Escape returns focus to the Diff control and preserves an unsent composer draft. No inference or account authorization was configured, and the draft was cleared without submitting it. Native inspection found overflowing long object/revision identifiers; wrapping was added, rebuilt and confirmed in the native panel. Supplementary Unicode path ordering is validated against the Worker's UTF-8 byte ordering rather than JavaScript UTF-16 ordering.
- Diff observations remain live, bounded views; submodules expose Gitlink commit changes only, with no fabricated lines for binary changes. File/line review comments, grouped agent feedback, other session apps and the remaining full issue requirements are separate unfinished work. No Rust source, protobuf or SQLite schema changed.

- Final frontend validation passes 52 files / 672 tests, type checking and production build (`/tmp/delidev-session-diff-frontend-final.log`). Native application-menu Quit leaves the detached private server alive as verified by authenticated CLI status; the exact Worker generation and private server are then explicitly stopped. Direct tray activation remains unverified. Generated frontend/client `dist` directories are removed before commit.

### Original Git review coordinates and read-only API (2026-09-28)

- Added a closed ordinary unified-diff parser and exact file/line anchors over the original Worker observation. Paths remain in the selected repository/query; old/new side numbers, hunk boundaries, original text and final-newline absence are retained. Up to 20 consecutive lines in one hunk and 8 KiB of selected context bind to the original diff revision and file-patch digest. Changed observations do not silently relocate anchors. Binary, mode-only, empty-file, symlink and submodule changes provide file anchors only.
- Actual temporary Git repositories pass paths containing spaces (including a literal ` b/`), Korean and supplementary Unicode, staged additions, empty-file creation, mode changes, deletions, binary changes and missing final newlines (`/tmp/delidev-review-anchors-focused.log`). Deterministic domain tests reject truncated/overlapping/combined/foreign/duplicate/unknown forms, invalid side/ranges, changed context and excessive selected content. Read-only Git now pins the C locale so Git's localized binary/EOF markers cannot change the coordinate grammar.
- `ReadSessionReviewContext` and `session review-context` use the same owner/client authorization and existing Worker observation channel. The RPC accepts no client patch and caps complete structured context at 1 MiB. Real server/Worker/CLI integration verifies coordinates and original diff revisions in both prepared repositories; Worker credentials and non-diff queries are rejected (`/tmp/delidev-review-context-focused.log`). Domain/workspace/server/CLI race tests pass (`/tmp/delidev-review-context-race.log`; 1.809s / 71.466s / 2.126s / 53.985s).
- Protocol formatting/lint and compatibility checks pass. Generated Go/TypeScript messages and Connect Query descriptors reproduce exactly on a second generation (`/tmp/delidev-review-context-generate-repeat.log`). Client tests pass 3 files / 41 tests and type checking (`/tmp/delidev-review-context-client.log`); frontend tests pass 52 files / 672 tests, type checking and build (`/tmp/delidev-review-context-frontend.log`). DeliDev-wide Go vet passes (`/tmp/delidev-review-context-vet.log`).
- These are coordinate/read evidence only: durable comment CRUD, explicit stale-state presentation, grouped agent feedback and the rest of issue #964 remain unfinished. No Rust source, database schema, native UI or real-account evidence is added in this increment.
